-- Obfuscated
local _a="ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/="
local _b={}
for i=1,#_a do _b[string.sub(_a,i,i)]=i-1 end
local function _dec(b)
    b=string.gsub(b,"[^".._a.."=]","")
    local out=""
    local acc=0
    local bits=0
    for i=1,#b do
        local c=string.sub(b,i,i)
        if c~="=" then
            local v=_b[c]
            acc=acc*64+v
            bits=bits+6
            if bits>=8 then
                bits=bits-8
                local byte=math.floor(acc/(2^bits))%256
                out=out..string.char(byte)
                acc=acc%(2^bits)
            end
        end
    end
    return out
end
local _k="StealEggKey99!"
local function _xor(s)
    local r={}
    local kl=#_k
    for i=1,#s do
        local c=string.byte(s,i)
        local k=string.byte(_k, ((i-1)%kl)+1)
        local d
        if bit32 and bit32.bxor then d=bit32.bxor(c,k)
        else
            d=0
            local a=c
            local b=k
            local p=1
            for _=1,8 do
                local abit=a%2
                local bbit=b%2
                if abit~=bbit then d=d+p end
                a=math.floor(a/2)
                b=math.floor(b/2)
                p=p*2
            end
        end
        r[i]=string.char(d)
    end
    return table.concat(r)
end

local _d=_dec(_p)
local _s=_xor(_d)
local f,err=loadstring(_s)
if f then local ok,er=pcall(f) if not ok then warn("obf err: "..tostring(er)) end else warn("loadstring err: "..tostring(err)) end
